##### Attraction/sexuality and childhood romance events
# by Silverswee(e)per

namespace = tianxia

# IDs 2901-3500 reserved

# TIANXIATODO: Pictures, frames
# TIANXIATODO: Trait effects
# TIANXIATODO: AI logic
# TIANXIATODO: Hook into on_actions
# TIANXIATODO: Handle betrothals when setting one up
# TIANXIATODO: Plausibility of pursuing someone, getting jealous, etc.
# TIANXIATODO: Rule out is_unattractive_trigger = yes when it comes to sex_attraction
# TIANXIATODO: Sanity check that follow-ups are sensible if someone has gotten opinion_crush/opinion_childhood_sweetheart or have broken up between events
# TIANXIATODO: Weight multipliers
# TIANXIATODO: Rule out a few more events if you've got a sexuality trait that'd mean you know you are(n't) attracted/interested in someone



##### Events for children

### Bedtime story - 4 to 8 years old

# You were told a story about a boy and a girl that fell in love - you've already realized that's your thing
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2901
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2901
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	hide_new = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 4
	max_age = 8
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			mother = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				at_location = ROOT
			}
			father = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				at_location = ROOT
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = told_bedtime_story
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_female = yes
				trait = romance_interest_male
			}
			AND = {
				is_female = no
				trait = romance_interest_female
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					mother = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
					}
				}
				mother = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					father = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
					}
				}
				father = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}
			}
		}
		set_character_flag = told_bedtime_story
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
			}
			create_same_sex_reference_character = yes
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				create_prev_opposite_sex_reference_character = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2901 # That's always nice!
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2907 } # Parent informed
		}
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2934 days = 20 random = 10 } # Follow-up
		}
	}
}

# You were told a story about a boy and a girl that fell in love - you realized that's your thing
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2902
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2902
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	hide_new = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 4
	max_age = 8
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			mother = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				at_location = ROOT
			}
			father = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				at_location = ROOT
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = told_bedtime_story
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_female = yes
				has_character_flag = romance_men
			}
			AND = {
				is_female = no
				has_character_flag = romance_women
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					mother = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
					}
				}
				mother = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					father = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
					}
				}
				father = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}
			}
		}
		set_character_flag = told_bedtime_story
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
			}
			create_same_sex_reference_character = yes
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				create_prev_opposite_sex_reference_character = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2902 # That was nice!
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = reveal_opposite_sex_romance_interest
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
				}
				clr_character_flag = romance_men
				add_trait = romance_interest_male
			}
			else = {
				clr_character_flag = romance_women
				add_trait = romance_interest_female
			}
			
			figured_things_out_check_effect = yes
		}
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2907 } # Parent informed
		}
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2934 days = 20 random = 10 } # Follow-up
		}
	}
}

	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2903

	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO

			AND = {
				is_female = yes
				NOR = {
					has_character_flag = no_romance_men
					trait = romance_interest_male
				}
			}
			AND = {
				is_female = no
				NOR = {
					has_character_flag = no_romance_women
					trait = romance_interest_female

			}
		}

						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
					}
				}
				father = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}

				ai = no
			}
			create_same_sex_reference_character = yes
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				create_prev_opposite_sex_reference_character = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2903 # I suppose that was a good story...
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2907 } # Parent informed
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2903 # Not sure how I felt about that...
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2908 } # Parent informed
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2903 # Bad story was bad!
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2909 } # Parent informed
		}
	}
}

# You were told a story about a boy and a girl that fell in love - you realized you kind of wished it was two boys/girls
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2906
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2906
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	hide_new = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 4
	max_age = 8
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			mother = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				at_location = ROOT
			}
			father = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				at_location = ROOT
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = told_bedtime_story
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_female = yes
				NOR = {
					has_character_flag = no_romance_men
					has_character_flag = romance_men
					trait = romance_interest_male
				}
				has_character_flag = romance_interest_female
			}
			AND = {
				is_female = no
				NOR = {
					has_character_flag = no_romance_women
					has_character_flag = romance_women
					trait = romance_interest_female
				}
				has_character_flag = romance_interest_male
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					mother = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
					}
				}
				mother = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					father = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
					}
				}
				father = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}
			}
		}
		set_character_flag = told_bedtime_story
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
			}
			create_same_sex_reference_character = yes
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				create_prev_opposite_sex_reference_character = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2906 # If only it had been two boys/girls...
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = reveal_same_sex_romance_interest
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
				}
				clr_character_flag = romance_women
				add_trait = romance_interest_female
			}
			else = {
				clr_character_flag = romance_men
				add_trait = romance_interest_male
			}
			
			figured_things_out_check_effect = yes
		}
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = reveal_no_opposite_sex_romance_interest
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
				}
				set_character_flag = no_romance_men
			}
			else = {
				set_character_flag = no_romance_women
			}
			
			figured_things_out_check_effect = yes
		}
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2908 } # Parent informed
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2906 # Yuck! It should have been two boys/girls!
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = reveal_same_sex_romance_interest
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
				}
				clr_character_flag = romance_women
				add_trait = romance_interest_female
			}
			else = {
				clr_character_flag = romance_men
				add_trait = romance_interest_male
			}
			
			figured_things_out_check_effect = yes
		}
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = reveal_no_opposite_sex_romance_interest
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
				}
				set_character_flag = no_romance_men
			}
			else = {
				set_character_flag = no_romance_women
			}
			
			figured_things_out_check_effect = yes
		}
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2909 } # Parent informed
		}
	}
}

# Parent told child seemed to like the story
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2907
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2907
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			opposite_sex_romance_interest_trait_trigger = yes
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2907 # Good!
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			opposite_sex_romance_interest_trait_trigger = no
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2907 # I guess they like boys/girls...
	}
}

# You were told a story about a boy and a girl, and they didn't fall in love for once - you already knew you like stories where they do fall in love
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2910
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2910
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	hide_new = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 4
	max_age = 8
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			mother = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				at_location = ROOT
			}
			father = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				at_location = ROOT
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = told_bedtime_story
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_female = yes
				trait = romance_interest_male
			}
			AND = {
				is_female = no
				trait = romance_interest_female
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					mother = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
					}
				}
				mother = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					father = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
					}
				}
				father = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}
			}
		}
		set_character_flag = told_bedtime_story
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
			}
			create_same_sex_reference_character = yes
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				create_prev_opposite_sex_reference_character = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2910 # But they should have fallen in love!
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2918 } # Parent informed
		}
	}
}

		}
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2924 } # Parent informed
		}
	}

# You were told a story about two boys/girls, and they ended up in love - you realized you'd have liked it if it was a boy and a girl
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2925
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2925
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	hide_new = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 4
	max_age = 8
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			mother = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				at_location = ROOT
				trait = romance_interest_female
			}
			father = {
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				at_location = ROOT
				trait = romance_interest_male
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = told_bedtime_story
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_female = yes
				has_character_flag = romance_men
				NOR = {
					has_character_flag = no_romance_women
					trait = romance_interest_female
				}
			}
			AND = {
				is_female = no
				has_character_flag = romance_women
				NOR = {
					has_character_flag = no_romance_men
					trait = romance_interest_male
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					mother = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
						trait = romance_interest_female
					}
				}
				mother = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}
			}
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					father = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						at_location = ROOT
						trait = romance_interest_male
					}
				}
				father = {
					save_event_target_as = storyteller_parent
				}
			}
		}
		set_character_flag = told_bedtime_story
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
			}
			create_same_sex_reference_character = yes
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				create_prev_same_sex_reference_character = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2925 # It should have been a boy and a girl!
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = reveal_no_same_sex_romance_interest
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
				}
				set_character_flag = no_romance_women
			}
			else = {
				set_character_flag = no_romance_men
			}
			
			figured_things_out_check_effect = yes
		}
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = reveal_opposite_sex_romance_interest
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
				}
				clr_character_flag = romance_men
				add_trait = romance_interest_male
			}
			else = {
				clr_character_flag = romance_women
				add_trait = romance_interest_female
			}
			
			figured_things_out_check_effect = yes
		}
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2933 } # Parent informed
		}
	}
}

### Bedtime story follow-up

# Child that liked bedtime story about a boy and a girl falling in love might ask parent that told it if they love the other parent
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2934
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2934
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			is_alive = yes
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			at_location = ROOT
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:storyteller_parent = {
					is_female = yes
				}
			}
			father_even_if_dead = {
				is_alive = yes
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			}
		}
		trigger_else = {
			mother_even_if_dead = {
				is_alive = yes
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:storyteller_parent = {
					is_female = yes
				}
			}
			father_even_if_dead = {
				save_event_target_as = other_parent
			}
		}
		else = {
			mother_even_if_dead = {
				save_event_target_as = other_parent
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2934 # I will ask them!
		
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2935 } # Storyteller gets event
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2934 # I don't want to bother them...
		
		prestige = -10
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc = "Conclave"
				}
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						add_trait = timid
					}
					50 = {
						add_trait = brooding
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_trait = shy
			}
		}
	}
}

# Mom/Dad, do you love dad/mom?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2935
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2935
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		event_target:other_parent = {
			save_event_target_as = changing_relationship_target
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:other_parent = {
				ROOT = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2935 # Of course I do!
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			fall_deeper_in_love_effect = yes
		}
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2936 } # Child informed of love
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:other_parent = {
				ROOT = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = no
				}
				ROOT_possibly_romantically_interested_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2935 # Come to think of it, I actually do love them!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				opposite_sex_sex_interest_trait_trigger = yes
				event_target:other_parent = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_ROOT_trigger = yes
					opposite_sex_sex_interest_trait_trigger = yes

					is_lover = event_target:other_parent
				}
				OR = {
					is_married = event_target:other_parent
					is_consort = event_target:other_parent
				}
			}
			add_lover_and_start_romance_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			start_romance_effect = yes
		}

			character_event = { id = tianxia.2936 } # Child informed of love
		}
	}

				ROOT = {

				}
			}
		}

				trigger = {

				}

					intrigue >= 10
				}

					factor = 2
					intrigue >= 15
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = child_believes_you
					FROM = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2940 } # Child falls for lie
					}
				}
			}
			50 = {
				mult_modifier=  {
					factor = 2
					FROM = {
						intrigue >= 5
					}
				}
				mult_modifier=  {
					factor = 2
					FROM = {
						intrigue >= 10
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = child_does_not_believe_you
					FROM = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2943 } # Child falls for lie
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = no
				}

# Other parent informed by child about love
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2937
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2937
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		event_target:storyteller_parent = {
			save_event_target_as = changing_relationship_target
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				ROOT = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2937 # And I love them, of course!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:storyteller_parent = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_ROOT_trigger = no
				}
			}
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2941 } # Inform the storyteller
			}
		}
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			fall_deeper_in_love_effect = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				ROOT = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = no
				}
				ROOT_possibly_romantically_interested_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2937 # Actually, I do love them!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				opposite_sex_sex_interest_trait_trigger = yes
				event_target:storyteller_parent = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_ROOT_trigger = yes
					opposite_sex_sex_interest_trait_trigger = yes
				}
				NOT = {
					is_lover = event_target:storyteller_parent
				}
				OR = {
					is_married = event_target:other_parent
					is_consort = event_target:other_parent
				}
			}
			add_lover_and_start_romance_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			start_romance_effect = yes
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:storyteller_parent = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_ROOT_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2938 } # Inform the storyteller
			}
		}
		else = {
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2941 } # Inform the storyteller
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				ROOT = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_PREV_trigger = no
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2937 # Well, I don't love them...
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:storyteller_parent = {
					has_romantic_feelings_towards_ROOT_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2939 } # Inform the storyteller
			}
		}
		else = {
			event_target:storyteller_parent = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2942 } # Inform the storyteller
			}
		}
	}
}

### Other child is pretty - opposite sex

# You're kind of finding another child pretty - you already knew you are sexually attracted to the opposite sex
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2947
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2947
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 6
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			has_character_flag = is_heterosexual
			has_character_flag = is_bisexual
			trait = heterosexual
			trait = bisexual
		}
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = pretty_child_event
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_female = yes
				has_character_flag = sex_attraction_male_known
			}
			AND = {
				is_female = no
				has_character_flag = sex_attraction_female_known
			}
		}
		top_liege = {
			any_realm_character = {
				is_adult = no
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
				age >= 4
				age_diff = {
					who = ROOT
					years <= 4
				}
				no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 400
							}
						}
					}
				}
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				is_attractive_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = pretty_child_event
		top_liege = {
			random_realm_character = {
				limit = {
					is_adult = no
					is_opposite_sex = ROOT
					age >= 4
					age_diff = {
						who = ROOT
						years <= 4
					}
					no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 400
								}
							}
						}
					}
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					is_attractive_trigger = yes
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					at_location = ROOT
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 100
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 200
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 300
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				save_event_target_as = attractive_child
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2947 # I'm interested in them!
		
		event_target:attractive_child = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2949 days = 15 random = 30 }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2947 # They're pretty, but I'm not interested in them...
	}
}

# General handling of other child being informed
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2949
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		FROM = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			is_adult = no
			same_realm = ROOT
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						has_character_flag = sex_attraction_male_known
					}
					AND = {
						is_female = no
						has_character_flag = sex_attraction_female_known
					}
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2950 } # Known attraction to the opposite sex
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = is_heterosexual
					has_character_flag = is_bisexual
					trait = heterosexual
					trait = bisexual
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 10
					}
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 14
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2951 } # Discovered attraction to the opposite sex
				}
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2952 } # Unsure about attraction to the opposite sex
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						has_character_flag = no_sex_attraction_male_known
					}
					AND = {
						is_female = no
						has_character_flag = no_sex_attraction_female_known
					}
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2953 } # Known lack of attraction to the opposite sex
		}
		else = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2952 } # Unsure about attraction to the opposite sex
				}
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 10
					}
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 14
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2954 } # Discovered lack of attraction to the opposite sex
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

### Other child is pretty - same sex

# You're kind of finding another child pretty - you already knew you are sexually attracted to the same sex
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2955
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2955
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 6
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			has_character_flag = is_homosexual
			has_character_flag = is_bisexual
			trait = homosexual
			trait = bisexual
		}
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = pretty_child_event
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_female = yes
				has_character_flag = sex_attraction_female_known
			}
			AND = {
				is_female = no
				has_character_flag = sex_attraction_male_known
			}
		}
		top_liege = {
			any_realm_character = {
				is_adult = no
				same_sex = ROOT
				age >= 4
				age_diff = {
					who = ROOT
					years <= 4
				}
				no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 400
							}
						}
					}
				}
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				is_attractive_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = pretty_child_event
		top_liege = {
			random_realm_character = {
				limit = {
					is_adult = no
					same_sex = ROOT
					age >= 4
					age_diff = {
						who = ROOT
						years <= 4
					}
					no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 400
								}
							}
						}
					}
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					is_attractive_trigger = yes
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					at_location = ROOT
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 100
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 200
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 300
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				save_event_target_as = attractive_child
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2955 # I'm interested in them!
		
		event_target:attractive_child = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2957 days = 15 random = 30 }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2955 # They're pretty, but I'm not interested in them...
	}
}

# General handling of other child being informed
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2957
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		FROM = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			is_adult = no
			same_realm = ROOT
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						has_character_flag = sex_attraction_female_known
					}
					AND = {
						is_female = no
						has_character_flag = sex_attraction_male_known
					}
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2958 } # Known attraction to the same sex
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = is_homosexual
					has_character_flag = is_bisexual
					trait = homosexual
					trait = bisexual
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 10
					}
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 14
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2959 } # Discovered attraction to the same sex
				}
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2960 } # Unsure about attraction to the same sex
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						has_character_flag = no_sex_attraction_female_known
					}
					AND = {
						is_female = no
						has_character_flag = no_sex_attraction_male_known
					}
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2961 } # Known lack of attraction to the same sex
		}
		else = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2960 } # Unsure about attraction to the same sex
				}
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 10
					}
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 14
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2962 } # Discovered lack of attraction to the same sex
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Hidden outcome of mutual sexual attraction and interest
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2963
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		FROM = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			is_adult = no
			same_realm = ROOT
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:attractive_child = {
					is_female = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					trait = romance_interest_female
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2964 } # Romantic interest and attraction known
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = romance_women
				}
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2965 } # Discovered romantic interest/attraction
					}
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2966 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = no_romance_women
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2967 } # Already sure about lack of romantic interest/attraction
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2966 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2968 } # Discovered lack of romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					trait = romance_interest_male
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2964 } # Romantic interest and attraction known
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = romance_men
				}
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2965 } # Discovered romantic interest/attraction
					}
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2966 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = no_romance_men
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2967 } # Already sure about lack of romantic interest/attraction
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2966 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2968 } # Discovered lack of romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Hidden outcome of being ignored
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2979
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		FROM = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			is_adult = no
			same_realm = ROOT
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:attractive_child = {
					is_female = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					trait = romance_interest_female
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2980 } # Romantic interest and attraction known
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = romance_women
				}
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2981 } # Discovered romantic interest/attraction
					}
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2982 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = no_romance_women
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2983 } # Already sure about lack of romantic interest/attraction
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2982 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2984 } # Discovered lack of romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					trait = romance_interest_male
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2980 } # Romantic interest and attraction known
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = romance_men
				}
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2981 } # Discovered romantic interest/attraction
					}
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2982 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = no_romance_men
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2983 } # Already sure about lack of romantic interest/attraction
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2982 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2984 } # Discovered lack of romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Hidden outcome of being avoided
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2995
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		FROM = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			is_adult = no
			same_realm = ROOT
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:attractive_child = {
					is_female = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					trait = romance_interest_female
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2996 } # Romantic interest and attraction known
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = romance_women
				}
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2997 } # Discovered romantic interest/attraction
					}
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2998 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = no_romance_women
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2999 } # Already sure about lack of romantic interest/attraction
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2998 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.3000 } # Discovered lack of romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					trait = romance_interest_male
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2996 } # Romantic interest and attraction known
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = romance_men
				}
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2997 } # Discovered romantic interest/attraction
					}
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2998 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = no_romance_men
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2999 } # Already sure about lack of romantic interest/attraction
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2998 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
					}
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 10
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							age >= 14
						}
						character_event = { id = tianxia.3000 } # Discovered lack of romantic interest/attraction
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

				else = {
					clr_character_flag = romance_men
					add_trait = romance_interest_male
				}
				
				figured_things_out_check_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_3002 # As if I loved them!
	}
}

# The other child had someone tell you to stay away - you've realized you don't have romantic feelings for their sex
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3006
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_3006
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3006 # I've realized romance isn't my thing...
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_opposite_sex = event_target:attractive_child
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = reveal_no_opposite_sex_romance_interest
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_female = yes
					}
					set_character_flag = no_romance_men
				}
				else = {
					set_character_flag = no_romance_women
				}
				
				figured_things_out_check_effect = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = reveal_no_same_sex_romance_interest
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_female = yes
					}
					set_character_flag = no_romance_women
				}
				else = {
					set_character_flag = no_romance_men
				}
				
				figured_things_out_check_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_3006 # I've realized romance isn't my thing... especially not with that person!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_opposite_sex = event_target:attractive_child
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = reveal_no_opposite_sex_romance_interest
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_female = yes
					}
					set_character_flag = no_romance_men
				}
				else = {
					set_character_flag = no_romance_women
				}
				
				figured_things_out_check_effect = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = reveal_no_same_sex_romance_interest
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_female = yes
					}
					set_character_flag = no_romance_women
				}
				else = {
					set_character_flag = no_romance_men
				}
				
				figured_things_out_check_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}



### Being in the company of another child is nice - opposite sex

# You're kind of finding another child's company nice - you already knew you are romantically attracted to the opposite sex
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3007
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_3007
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 6
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_female = yes
				trait = romance_interest_male
			}
			AND = {
				is_female = no
				trait = romance_interest_female
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = nice_child_event
		}
		top_liege = {
			any_realm_character = {
				is_adult = no
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
				age >= 4
				age_diff = {
					who = ROOT
					years <= 4
				}
				no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 400
							}
						}
					}
				}
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				is_attractive_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = nice_child_event
		top_liege = {
			random_realm_character = {
				limit = {
					is_adult = no
					is_opposite_sex = ROOT
					age >= 4
					age_diff = {
						who = ROOT
						years <= 4
					}
					no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 400
								}
							}
						}
					}
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					is_attractive_trigger = yes
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					at_location = ROOT
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 100
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 200
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					location = {
						ROOT = {
							location = {
								distance = {
									where = PREVPREV
									value <= 300
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				save_event_target_as = attractive_child
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3007 # I'm interested in them!
		
		event_target:attractive_child = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3009 days = 15 random = 30 }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_3007 # They're nice, but I'm not interested in them...
	}
}

# General handling of other child being informed
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3009
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		FROM = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			is_adult = no
			same_realm = ROOT
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						trait = romance_interest_male
					}
					AND = {
						is_female = no
						trait = romance_interest_female
					}
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3010 } # Known attraction to the opposite sex
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						has_character_flag = romance_men
					}
					AND = {
						is_female = no
						has_character_flag = romance_women
					}
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 10
					}
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 14
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3011 } # Discovered attraction to the opposite sex
				}
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3012 } # Unsure about attraction to the opposite sex
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						has_character_flag = no_romance_men
					}
					AND = {
						is_female = no
						has_character_flag = no_romance_women
					}
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3013 } # Known lack of attraction to the opposite sex
		}
		else = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3012 } # Unsure about attraction to the opposite sex
				}
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 10
					}
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 14
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3014 } # Discovered lack of attraction to the opposite sex
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# General handling of other child being informed
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3017
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		FROM = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			is_adult = no
			same_realm = ROOT
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						trait = romance_interest_female
					}
					AND = {
						is_female = no
						trait = romance_interest_male
					}
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3018 } # Known attraction to the same sex
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						has_character_flag = romance_women
					}
					AND = {
						is_female = no
						has_character_flag = romance_men
					}
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 10
					}
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 14
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3019 } # Discovered attraction to the same sex
				}
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3020 } # Unsure about attraction to the same sex
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						has_character_flag = no_romance_women
					}
					AND = {
						is_female = no
						has_character_flag = no_romance_men
					}
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3021 } # Known lack of attraction to the same sex
		}
		else = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3020 } # Unsure about attraction to the same sex
				}
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 10
					}
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						age >= 14
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.3022 } # Discovered lack of attraction to the same sex
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Hidden event to handle outcome of being approached
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3025
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		FROM = {
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			is_adult = no
			same_realm = ROOT
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					is_female = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					trait = romance_interest_female
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.3026 } # Romantic interest and attraction known
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = no_romance_women
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.3028 } # Already sure about lack of romantic interest/attraction
			}
			else = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.3027 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					trait = romance_interest_male
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.3026 } # Romantic interest and attraction known
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = no_romance_men
				}
				character_event = { id = tianxia.3028 } # Already sure about lack of romantic interest/attraction
			}
			else = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.3027 } # Unsure about romantic interest/attraction
			}
		}
	}
}

### Fairest in the realm

# Someone of the opposite sex is considered (among) the fairest/most handsome in the realm, and you realize you can see it
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3035
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_3035
	picture = GFX_evt_garden # TIANXIATODO
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy # TIANXIATODO
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_childhood_pulse -> random_events -> attraction_sexuality_discovery
	
	min_age = 10
	max_age = 15
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		any_realm_character = {
			is_adult = yes
			is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
			location = {
				ROOT = {
					location = {
						distance = {
							where = PREVPREV
							value <= 400
						}
					}
				}
			}
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			has_fair_trait_trigger = yes
			is_unattractive_trigger = no # Not maimed/sick/etc.
		}
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = fairest_of_them_all_events
		}
		OR = {
			has_character_flag = is_heterosexual
			has_character_flag = is_bisexual
			trait = heterosexual
			trait = bisexual
		}
		NOR = {
			AND = {
				is_female = yes
				has_character_flag = sex_attraction_male_known
			}
			AND = {
				is_female = no
				has_character_flag = sex_attraction_female_known
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = fairest_of_them_all_events
		random_realm_character = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
				no_incest_concern_ROOT_trigger = yes
				location = {
					ROOT = {
						location = {
							distance = {
								where = PREVPREV
								value <= 400
							}
						}
					}
				}
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				has_fair_trait_trigger = yes
				is_unattractive_trigger = no # Not maimed/sick/etc.
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				age < 20
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				age < 25
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				age < 30
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				age < 35
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				age < 40
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = attractive_character
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3035 # They're attractive, and I'm into them!
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = reveal_opposite_sex_sex_attraction
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
				}
				set_character_flag = sex_attraction_male_known
			}
			else = {
				set_character_flag = sex_attraction_female_known
			}
			
			figured_things_out_check_effect = yes
		}
		
		opinion = {
			name = opinion_infatuated
			who = event_target:attractive_character
		 }
		 
		 event_target:attractive_character = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3037 }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3035 # They're attractive, but I'm not interested in them...
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = reveal_opposite_sex_sex_attraction
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
				}
				set_character_flag = sex_attraction_male_known
			}
			else = {
				set_character_flag = sex_attraction_female_known
			}
			
			figured_things_out_check_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

	id = tianxia.3047
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_3047

	max_age = 13
	
	prisoner = no

		OR = {
			has_character_flag = is_bisexual
			has_character_flag = is_heterosexual
			trait = bisexual
			trait = heterosexual
		}
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = puberty_events
		}

		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_3047 # I like what I'm seeing!

			limit = {
				NOR = {
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						has_character_flag = sex_attraction_male_known
					}

						is_female = no

# Hidden setup
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.3059
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = puberty_events
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						has_character_flag = sex_interest_male
					}
					AND = {
						is_female = no
						has_character_flag = sex_interest_female
					}
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3060 } # You already knew you were interested 
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = is_bisexual
					has_character_flag = is_heterosexual
					trait = bisexual
					trait = heterosexual
					has_character_flag = asexual_opposite
					has_character_flag = asexual_both
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3061 } # You figure out you are interested
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						has_character_flag = no_sex_interest_male_known
					}
					AND = {
						is_female = no
						has_character_flag = no_sex_interest_female_known
					}
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3062 } # You already had figured out your lack of interest
		}
		
		else = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.3063 } # You figure out your lack of interst
		}
	}
}
